[Peakshire, Trail]
This is a small trail running vaguely north and south, heading from Erirt
Road towards the bog near the Shartans. Stubby trees, their leaves a dull
green color, line both sides of the small path. The peaks of the Shartans 
are snowy and bright, looming overhead. The ground is soft and spongy 
underfoot. The boggy ground starts to the north and northeast, continuing 
until the rocky feet of the Shartans.
You see Trieste, Sy, Wolfbane and a bog ooze. 
Obvious exits: north, northeast.

Lord of Fayd. His clearly wolfling features are highlighted by warm hazel eyes
that hint at a gentleness hidden by his rugged exterior.
He is wearing a ruby studded silver ring of the Crimson Band, a blackened
elkyri helm emblazoned with a screaming silver eagle, some blackened leather
gloves each with a silver dragon embroidered on the top, a heavy gold wedding
ring of a majestic crimson eagle gently grasping a blushing silver rose in its
talon, a finely crafted crimson tabard embroidered with the gleaming silver
eagle of the Crimson Band, some silver edged elkyri bracers engraved with a
battle scene from the war with Siryx, some blackened banded scarlet boots, a
dark brown leather belt with a large brass buckle of a noble eagle in front of
two crossed swords, some crimson steel bracers inset with a pair of small
emeralds in each one, some heavy crimson thigh guards polished to a high
lustre, a heavy braided gold torc capped with spheres of polished amber, a
black and red cape with a large eagle stitched into the back and some blackened
deeplander armor emblazoned with the flying eagle of the Crimson Band upon the
chest.
He is wielding a finely crafted albescent longsword with fiery randar forming a
pommel shaped into a screaming eagle's head and a fiery randar papenheimer.
He is behind a wall of force.
He appears to be healthy.
He has a nicked, grazed and singed head, a nicked and singed body, a nicked,
banged-up and grazed back, a nicked and grazed right arm, a nicked, bludgeoned
and grazed left arm, a nicked and grazed right leg, a lacerated left leg, a
nicked right hand and a nicked and grazed left hand.

> look tri
Silvery white hair falls in a shining curtain to Trieste's slender hips, broken
only by the delicate ebony points of her ears. Luminous green eyes accent
finely drawn mardhrel features, their fire belieing her restrained posture.
She is wearing a pale ivory anklet, a sheer silvery black robe that glistens
with an iridescence of subtle beauty, a gas mask, a finely engraved silver ring
set with a single black pearl, a sterling silver ring bearing a worn engraving
and set with an icy opal, some smooth black leather gloves that fit like a
second skin, a delicate silvery onyx ring inset with a single icy opal, a
delicate silver chain belt that gleams against her ebony skin and flashes when
she moves, some fine aelfen chain mail delicately wrought to resemble
spiderwebs shimmering in moonlight, some soft black leather boots embroidered
with a silver rose enmeshed in an ebony web, a finely crafted aelfen helm of
blackened links of chain covering her silver hair and a black velvet cape.
She radiates with magical strength.
She is protected by a ghostly, hovering shield.
She is being guarded by Wolfbane.
She appears to be healthy.
She has a nicked, slightly punctured and scorched head, a banged-up, slightly
punctured and scorched body, a lacerated and grazed back, a fractured and
grazed right arm, a nicked, banged-up, slightly punctured and singed left arm,
a banged-up and slightly punctured right leg, a banged-up and grazed left leg,

This is a small trail running vaguely north and south, heading from Erirt
Road towards the bog near the Shartans. Stubby trees, their leaves a dull
green color, line both sides of the small path. The peaks of the Shartans 
are snowy and bright, looming overhead. The ground is soft and spongy 
underfoot. The boggy ground starts to the north and northeast, continuing 
until the rocky feet of the Shartans.

Before you stands Wolfbane Crag, Guildmaster of the Crimson Band and first
Lord of Fayd. His clearly wolfling features are highlighted by warm hazel eyes
that hint at a gentleness hidden by his rugged exterior.
He is wearing a ruby studded silver ring of the Crimson Band, a blackened
elkyri helm emblazoned with a screaming silver eagle, some blackened leather
gloves each with a silver dragon embroidered on the top, a heavy gold wedding
ring of a majestic crimson eagle gently grasping a blushing silver rose in its
talon, a finely crafted crimson tabard embroidered with the gleaming silver
eagle of the Crimson Band, some silver edged elkyri bracers engraved with a
battle scene from the war with Siryx, some blackened banded scarlet boots, a
dark brown leather belt with a large brass buckle of a noble eagle in front of
two crossed swords, some crimson steel bracers inset with a pair of small
emeralds in each one, some heavy crimson thigh guards polished to a high
lustre, a heavy braided gold torc capped with spheres of polished amber, a
black and red cape with a large eagle stitched into the back and some blackened
deeplander armor emblazoned with the flying eagle of the Crimson Band upon the
chest.
He is wielding a finely crafted albescent longsword with fiery randar forming a
pommel shaped into a screaming eagle's head and a fiery randar papenheimer.
He is behind a wall of force.
He looks a bit under the weather.
He has a nicked, banged-up, grazed and singed head, a nicked and singed body, a
nicked, banged-up and grazed back, a nicked and grazed right arm, a nicked,
bludgeoned and grazed left arm, a nicked and grazed right leg, a lacerated left

Before you stands Wolfbane Crag, Guildmaster of the Crimson Band and first
Lord of Fayd. His clearly wolfling features are highlighted by warm hazel eyes
that hint at a gentleness hidden by his rugged exterior.
He is wearing a ruby studded silver ring of the Crimson Band, a blackened
elkyri helm emblazoned with a screaming silver eagle, some blackened leather
gloves each with a silver dragon embroidered on the top, a heavy gold wedding
ring of a majestic crimson eagle gently grasping a blushing silver rose in its
talon, a finely crafted crimson tabard embroidered with the gleaming silver
eagle of the Crimson Band, some silver edged elkyri bracers engraved with a
battle scene from the war with Siryx, some blackened banded scarlet boots, a
dark brown leather belt with a large brass buckle of a noble eagle in front of
two crossed swords, some crimson steel bracers inset with a pair of small
emeralds in each one, some heavy crimson thigh guards polished to a high
lustre, a heavy braided gold torc capped with spheres of polished amber, a
black and red cape with a large eagle stitched into the back and some blackened
deeplander armor emblazoned with the flying eagle of the Crimson Band upon the
chest.
He is wielding a finely crafted albescent longsword with fiery randar forming a
pommel shaped into a screaming eagle's head and a fiery randar papenheimer.
He is behind a wall of force.
He appears to be healthy.

[Teeth of Shartan, streambed]
A frothing mountain stream surges down the mountains, rolling over the mossy
rocks and debris in its bed with reckless abandon. Downstream from here, the
massive rock walls of the valley close tightly around the stream. To the north,
however, the valley widens somewhat. Countless eons of traffic by all sorts of
travellers have worn a fine trail which winds northward through the the valley
towards towering mountain peaks. Another path of loose rubble leads up the
steep valley wall.
You see Sy and a water elemental. A huge greatsword, a huge pearl, a giant
warhammer, a chipped bloodstone, a giant tooth, a giant warhammer, a giant
beard, some gold coins, some silver coins, some copper coins and a giant beard
are here.
Obvious exits: north, up.

A frothing mountain stream surges down the mountains, rolling over the mossy
rocks and debris in its bed with reckless abandon. Downstream from here, the
massive rock walls of the valley close tightly around the stream. To the north,
however, the valley widens somewhat. Countless eons of traffic by all sorts of
travellers have worn a fine trail which winds northward through the the valley
towards towering mountain peaks. Another path of loose rubble leads up the
steep valley wall.

Pale skin and grim features are a perplexing contrast to the sparkle of life
in his eyes, one pupil being silver the other pale green. A mane of thick
black dreadlocks intertwined with strands of copper wire frame his face.
He is wearing a dark crystal medallion, a karhadian coat, a hand-tooled leather
quiver, a steel nosering, a black leather satchel, a technological bracelet,
some thin weighted plastic armor with a golden eagle emblazoned on the
breastplate, a wide blue plastic helm with a golden eagle inscribed over the
visor, a studded collar and some deeplander boots.
He is wielding a shiny plasteel greatbow equipped with a high tech laser
bowsight.
He has a tattoo of a serpent biting it's own tail on the back of his neck.
He is behind a wall of force.
A translucent red sphere surrounds him.
He is being guarded by a water elemental.
He looks a bit under the weather.

[Teeth of Shartan, streambed]
A frothing mountain stream surges down the mountains, rolling over the mossy
rocks and debris in its bed with reckless abandon. Downstream from here, the
massive rock walls of the valley close tightly around the stream. To the north,
however, the valley widens somewhat. Countless eons of traffic by all sorts of
travellers have worn a fine trail which winds northward through the the valley
towards towering mountain peaks. Another path of loose rubble leads up the
steep valley wall.

warhammer, a giant beard, a huge greatsword, a citrine, a chipped bloodstone, a

towards towering mountain peaks. Another path of loose rubble leads up the

[Teeth of Shartan, streambed]
A frothing mountain stream surges down the mountains, rolling over the mossy
rocks and debris in its bed with reckless abandon. Downstream from here, the
massive rock walls of the valley close tightly around the stream. To the north,
however, the valley widens somewhat. Countless eons of traffic by all sorts of
travellers have worn a fine trail which winds northward through the the valley
towards towering mountain peaks. Another path of loose rubble leads up the
steep valley wall.

[Physicians' Herb Shop]
The air in this room has a confusion of different odors. The smells come
from the various herbs that hang from the ceiling, or lie in bunches on
top of shelves. Behind the counter there are stacks of jars containing
the salvaged anatomy from many of  Andor's creatures. Various guild
members browse through the store, and engage in heated discussions about
the medicinal properties of various ingredients. There is a small sign
hanging on the wall behind the counter that lists the items for sale. A
small uquart barrel stands in the corner.
